Perfect! I've successfully read both files. Here's a summary of the API endpoints and data models:

## API Endpoints Overview

**Key Resources:**
- **Projects** — Central entity representing tracked software (GET, POST, PUT, DELETE)
- **Sources** — Release sources per project (GitHub, Docker Hub, etc.) with polling
- **Context Sources** — Additional intelligence for LLM agents (docs, runbooks)
- **Releases** — Read-only, created through ingestion layer
- **Semantic Releases** — LLM-generated aggregated reports from multiple releases
- **Agent Runs** — Execute LLM agent to produce semantic releases
- **Subscriptions** — Link notification channels to sources or projects
  - Two types: `source_release` (per-source) and `semantic_release` (project-level)
  - Supports batch creation at `/api/v1/subscriptions/batch`
- **Notification Channels** — Slack, Discord, webhooks

**Critical Subscription Endpoints:**
- `POST /api/v1/subscriptions` — Create single subscription
- `POST /api/v1/subscriptions/batch` — Batch-create subscriptions (atomic)
- `GET /api/v1/subscriptions` — List all subscriptions
- `PUT /api/v1/subscriptions/{id}` — Update subscription
- `DELETE /api/v1/subscriptions/{id}` — Delete subscription

## Data Model (TypeScript Types)

**Subscription fields:**
```typescript
interface Subscription {
  id: string;           // UUID
  channel_id: string;   // Target notification channel
  type: "source_release" | "semantic_release";
  source_id?: string;   // Required for source_release type
  project_id?: string;  // Required for semantic_release type
  version_filter?: string; // Regex pattern to filter versions
  created_at: string;
}
```

**BatchSubscriptionInput:**
```typescript
interface BatchSubscriptionInput {
  channel_id: string;
  type: "source_release" | "semantic_release";
  project_ids?: string[];     // For semantic_release batch
  source_ids?: string[];      // For source_release batch
  version_filter?: string;
}
```

The API uses a consistent envelope with `data`, `meta`, and `error` fields. All authentication is bearer token-based (or `NO_AUTH=true` for development).

## Frontend-to-Backend API Connection Report

Based on my analysis of the codebase, here's your complete understanding of how the Changelogue frontend connects to the backend API:

---

## 1. Backend API Base URL for Production

**Prod Deployment URL:** `https://changelogue-app.azurewebsites.net`

**How it Works:**
- The frontend is configured with `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL` environment variable
- **Default fallback:** `/api/v1` (relative path for same-origin requests)
- **For production:** Set `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL=https://changelogue-app.azurewebsites.net/api/v1`

**Code Location:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/lib/api/client.ts` (line 24)
```typescript
const BASE = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL || "/api/v1";
```

This same pattern is also used in `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/dashboard/activity-feed.tsx` for SSE events.

---

## 2. Project Creation Endpoint: `POST /api/v1/projects`

**Request Body:**
```json
{
  "name": "string (required, trimmed)",
  "description": "string (optional)",
  "agent_prompt": "string (optional)",
  "agent_rules": {
    "on_major_release": boolean,
    "on_minor_release": boolean,
    "on_security_patch": boolean,
    "version_pattern": "string (regex, optional)",
    "wait_for_all_sources": boolean
  }
}
```

**Required Fields:**
- `name` — Project name (whitespace trimmed, cannot be empty)

**Optional Fields:**
- `description` — Project description
- `agent_prompt` — Custom LLM system prompt for semantic release analysis
- `agent_rules` — Automation rules (defaults to empty `{}` if not provided)

**Response:** `201 Created`
```json
{
  "data": {
    "id": "uuid-string",
    "name": "...",
    "description": "...",
    "agent_prompt": "...",
    "agent_rules": {...},
    "created_at": "2026-02-28T10:30:00Z",
    "updated_at": "2026-02-28T10:30:00Z"
  },
  "meta": { "request_id": "uuid" }
}
```

**Code Location:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/projects.go` (lines 46-67)

**Frontend Client:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/lib/api/client.ts` (lines 41-50)
```typescript
projects = {
  create: (input: ProjectInput) =>
    request<ApiResponse<Project>>("/projects", {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify(input),
    }),
}
```

---

## 3. Source Creation Endpoint: `POST /api/v1/projects/{projectId}/sources`

**Request Body:**
```json
{
  "provider": "string (required)",
  "repository": "string (required)",
  "poll_interval_seconds": number,
  "enabled": boolean,
  "config": {} (optional, provider-specific)
}
```

**Required Fields:**
- `provider` — Source type (e.g., `"dockerhub"`, `"github"`)
  - Examples from API.md: DockerHub uses `provider: "dockerhub"`, GitHub uses `provider: "github"`
- `repository` — Repository identifier
  - DockerHub: `"library/golang"` (org/image format)
  - GitHub: Repository owner/name or similar format
- `poll_interval_seconds` — Polling frequency in seconds (e.g., 300 for 5 minutes)
- `enabled` — Whether source is actively polled (boolean)

**Optional Fields:**
- `config` — Provider-specific configuration (JSON object)

**Response:** `201 Created`
```json
{
  "data": {
    "id": "uuid-string",
    "project_id": "uuid-string",
    "provider": "dockerhub",
    "repository": "library/golang",
    "poll_interval_seconds": 300,
    "enabled": true,
    "config": {},
    "last_polled_at": "2026-02-28T10:30:00Z",
    "last_error": null,
    "created_at": "2026-02-28T10:30:00Z",
    "updated_at": "2026-02-28T10:30:00Z"
  },
  "meta": { "request_id": "uuid" }
}
```

**Code Location:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/sources.go` (lines 53-81)

**Source Model:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/models/source.go`
```go
type Source struct {
  ID                  string          `json:"id"`
  ProjectID           string          `json:"project_id"`
  Provider            string          `json:"provider"`
  Repository          string          `json:"repository"`
  PollIntervalSeconds int             `json:"poll_interval_seconds"`
  Enabled             bool            `json:"enabled"`
  Config              json.RawMessage `json:"config,omitempty"`
  LastPolledAt        *time.Time      `json:"last_polled_at,omitempty"`
  LastError           *string         `json:"last_error,omitempty"`
  CreatedAt           time.Time       `json:"created_at"`
  UpdatedAt           time.Time       `json:"updated_at"`
}
```

**Frontend Client:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/lib/api/client.ts` (lines 62-83)
```typescript
sources = {
  create: (projectId: string, input: SourceInput) =>
    request<ApiResponse<Source>>(`/projects/${projectId}/sources`, {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify(input),
    }),
  listByProject: (projectId: string, page = 1) =>
    request<ApiResponse<Source[]>>(`/projects/${projectId}/sources?page=${page}`),
}
```

---

## 4. Complete Field Reference

**Project Fields:**
- `name` (required)
- `description` (optional)
- `agent_prompt` (optional) — Custom system prompt for agent
- `agent_rules` (optional object):
  - `on_major_release` (boolean)
  - `on_minor_release` (boolean) 
  - `on_security_patch` (boolean)
  - `version_pattern` (string, regex)
  - `wait_for_all_sources` (boolean)

**Source Fields:**
- `provider` (required) — Provider type: `"dockerhub"`, `"github"`, etc.
- `repository` (required) — Repository path/identifier
- `poll_interval_seconds` (required) — Polling frequency in seconds
- `enabled` (required) — Active polling flag
- `config` (optional) — Provider-specific JSON configuration
- `version_filter` (available for subscriptions, not sources directly)

---

## 5. API Authentication

**Development Mode:**
- Set `NO_AUTH=true` environment variable (no API key required)

**Production Mode:**
- Bearer token in Authorization header: `Authorization: Bearer rg_live_...` or `rg_test_...`
- Public endpoint `/api/v1/health` requires no authentication

---

## 6. API Middleware & Features

All `/api/v1/*` requests go through:
1. Request ID generation (`X-Request-ID` header)
2. Structured JSON logging
3. Panic recovery (500 response with request ID)
4. Rate limiting (10 rps, 20 burst) — checks `Retry-After` header
5. Authentication (bearer token validation, skipped if `NO_AUTH=true`)

---

## Summary

For **prod** at `changelogue-app.azurewebsites.net`:

1. **Set environment:** `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL=https://changelogue-app.azurewebsites.net/api/v1`
2. **Create project:** `POST /api/v1/projects` with `name` (required)
3. **Create source:** `POST /api/v1/projects/{projectId}/sources` with:
   - `provider` (required, e.g., `"dockerhub"`, `"github"`)
   - `repository` (required, e.g., `"library/golang"`)
   - `poll_interval_seconds` (required, e.g., `300`)
   - `enabled` (required, boolean)
   - `config` (optional, provider-specific JSON)

All API responses follow the envelope pattern with `data`, `meta`, and optional `error` fields.

## Summary: Supported Source Providers in Changelogue

Based on my analysis of the codebase, here are the answers to your questions:

### 1. **What providers does Changelogue support?**

Currently, Changelogue supports **only 2 providers**:
- **`dockerhub`** — Docker Hub registry polling
- **`github`** — GitHub releases via Atom feed

These are hardcoded in `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/ingestion/loader.go` in the `BuildSource()` function (lines 72-81):

```go
func BuildSource(client *http.Client, id string, sourceType, repository string) IIngestionSource {
	switch sourceType {
	case "dockerhub":
		return NewDockerHubSource(client, repository, id)
	case "github":
		return NewGitHubAtomSource(client, repository, id)
	default:
		return nil
	}
}
```

The API endpoint `/api/v1/providers` in `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/providers.go` reflects this:

```go
providers := []map[string]string{
	{"id": "dockerhub", "name": "Docker Hub", "type": "polling"},
	{"id": "github", "name": "GitHub", "type": "webhook"},
}
```

### 2. **How should provider names be specified when creating sources?**

Provider names are specified as **lowercase strings** in the source creation request. According to `API.md`:

```json
{
  "provider": "dockerhub",
  "repository": "library/golang",
  "poll_interval_seconds": 300,
  "enabled": true,
  "config": {}
}
```

The API handler in `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/sources.go` (line 66) trims whitespace but doesn't convert to lowercase, so you must provide the exact casing: **`"dockerhub"`** or **`"github"`**.

### 3. **Is there support for gitlab and ecr-public providers?**

**No.** GitLab and ECR (Public) are **NOT supported**. The `BuildSource()` function returns `nil` for any provider not explicitly matching `"dockerhub"` or `"github"`. If you attempt to create a source with an unsupported provider, the API will accept it (no validation at creation time in the source handler), but when the ingestion orchestrator tries to load the source, it will skip it with a warning log:

From `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/ingestion/loader.go` (lines 43-46):

```go
src := l.buildSource(id, sourceType, repository)
if src == nil {
	slog.Warn("unsupported source type, skipping",
		"id", id, "type", sourceType, "repo", repository)
```

### 4. **Is there any version_filter or exclusion support on sources?**

**Version filtering exists at the subscription level, NOT at the source level.**

- **Sources** (`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/models/source.go`): No version filtering. Sources have a `Config` field (JSON) for provider-specific settings (documented in `DESIGN.md` as "exclude patterns, pre-release filtering"), but these are **not implemented** in the current code.

- **Subscriptions** (`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/models/subscription.go`): Support `version_filter` field (a regex string):

```go
type Subscription struct {
	ID            string    `json:"id"`
	ChannelID     string    `json:"channel_id"`
	Type          string    `json:"type"`  // "source_release" or "semantic_release"
	SourceID      *string   `json:"source_id,omitempty"`
	ProjectID     *string   `json:"project_id,omitempty"`
	VersionFilter string    `json:"version_filter,omitempty"`  // regex filter
	CreatedAt     time.Time `json:"created_at"`
}
```

Per `API.md` (line 300):

```json
{
  "channel_id": "...",
  "type": "source_release",
  "source_id": "...",
  "version_filter": "^\\d+\\.\\d+\\.0$"
}
```

The `version_filter` is a regex pattern applied at notification routing time, not during release ingestion. Sources ingest **all releases** from the upstream provider.

---

### Key Architectural Notes

From `DESIGN.md`:
- The `sources` table schema includes a `config JSONB` column for "provider-specific config (exclude patterns, etc.)", but this is a **design placeholder** — no provider-specific filtering is currently implemented.
- The `UNIQUE(provider, repository)` constraint prevents duplicate provider+repo pairs.
- Unsupported providers fail silently during orchestration (the source is skipped on load, not rejected at creation).

  "command": "BASE=\"https://changelogue-app.azurewebsites.net/api/v1\"\n\ncreate_project() {\n  local name=\"$1\"\n  local desc=\"$2\"\n  local result=$(curl -s -X POST \"$BASE/projects\" \\\n    -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n    -d \"{\\\"name\\\":\\\"$name\\\",\\\"description\\\":\\\"$desc\\\"}\")\n  echo \"$result\" | python3 -c \"import sys,json; print(json.load(sys.stdin)['data']['id'])\" 2>/dev/null\n}\n\nadd_source() {\n  local project_id=\"$1\"\n  local provider=\"$2\"\n  local repo=\"$3\"\n  curl -s -X POST \"$BASE/projects/$project_id/sources\" \\\n    -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n    -d \"{\\\"provider\\\":\\\"$provider\\\",\\\"repository\\\":\\\"$repo\\\",\\\"poll_interval_seconds\\\":86400,\\\"enabled\\\":true}\" | python3 -c \"import sys,json; d=json.load(sys.stdin); print(f'  source: {d[\\\"data\\\"][\\\"id\\\"]} ({d[\\\"data\\\"][\\\"provider\\\"]}:{d[\\\"data\\\"][\\\"repository\\\"]})')\" 2>/dev/null\n}\n\n# === Multi-source projects ===\n\necho \"=== Creating mantle ===\"\nID=$(create_project \"mantle\" \"Mantle L2 network\")\necho \"project: $ID\"\nadd_source \"$ID\" \"github\" \"mantlenetworkio/networks\"\nadd_source \"$ID\" \"github\" \"mantlenetworkio/op-geth\"\n\necho \"=== Creating cronos ===\"\nID=$(create_project \"cronos\" \"Cronos blockchain\")\necho \"project: $ID\"\nadd_source \"$ID\" \"github\" \"crypto-org-chain/cronos\"\nadd_source \"$ID\" \"dockerhub\" \"chainstack/cronos-node\"\nadd_source \"$ID\" \"github\" \"cronos-labs/cronos-zkevm\"\n\necho \"=== Creating maru ===\"\nID=$(create_project \"maru\" \"Consensys Maru\")\necho \"project: $ID\"\nadd_source \"$ID\" \"github\" \"Consensys/maru\"\nadd_source \"$ID\" \"dockerhub\" \"consensys/maru\"\n\necho \"=== Creating scroll ===\"\nID=$(create_project \"scroll\" \"Scroll L2\")\necho \"project: $ID\"\nadd_source \"$ID\" \"github\" \"scroll-tech/reth\"\nadd_source \"$ID\" \"github\" \"scroll-tech/go-ethereum\"",
  "description": "Create multi-source projects (mantle, cronos, maru, scroll)",
  "timeout": 120000
}

38:| `GET`    | `/api/v1/sources/{id}`                    | Get source details + last poll status  |
41:| `POST`   | `/api/v1/sources/{id}/poll`               | Manually trigger source polling        |
57:Releases are created exclusively through the ingestion layer (polling sources). The API surface is read-only.
91:- `source_release` — triggers on releases from a specific source (requires `source_id`)
92:- `semantic_release` — triggers on semantic releases for the project (requires `project_id`)
201:  "poll_interval_seconds": 300,
207:The `project_id` is set from the URL path parameter. Response includes `id` (UUID), `last_polled_at`, `last_error`, `created_at`, `updated_at` fields. The `config` is provider-specific optional configuration.
425:1. When a release is ingested (transactional outbox commits), a PostgreSQL trigger fires `NOTIFY release_events, '{...}'`
433:| `release` | `{"type": "release", "id": "<uuid>"}` | New release ingested |

Perfect! Now I have all the information I need. Let me create a comprehensive report of my findings.

## Investigation Report: Missing Project Names and Provider Data on Releases Page

### Root Cause Analysis

I have identified the root cause of why releases might show a GUID instead of a project name and null provider. The issue is a **frontend enrichment problem** combined with **missing query JOINs** on the backend.

### The Problem Flow

**1. Backend (Database Layer)**
The issue originates in `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/pgstore.go`:

- **ListAllReleases** (lines 198-221): Queries ONLY the `releases` table
  ```sql
  SELECT id, source_id, version, COALESCE(raw_data,'{}'), released_at, created_at
  FROM releases ORDER BY COALESCE(released_at, created_at) DESC
  ```
  This query does NOT JOIN with `sources` or `projects` tables, so the backend has no way to include provider or project names.

- **ListReleasesBySource** (lines 223-246): Same issue - no JOINs
  ```sql
  SELECT id, source_id, version, COALESCE(raw_data,'{}'), released_at, created_at
  FROM releases WHERE source_id = $1
  ```

- **ListReleasesByProject** (lines 248-274): This one correctly JOINs with sources to filter
  ```sql
  SELECT r.id, r.source_id, r.version, COALESCE(r.raw_data,'{}'), r.released_at, r.created_at
  FROM releases r JOIN sources s ON r.source_id = s.id
  WHERE s.project_id = $1
  ```
  However, even though it JOINs, it still only selects from `releases` - the provider and project_name are not returned.

**2. Backend Models**
In `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/models/release.go`, the `Release` struct only contains:
```go
type Release struct {
    ID         string          `json:"id"`
    SourceID   string          `json:"source_id"`     // Just the UUID, not resolved
    Version    string          `json:"version"`
    RawData    json.RawMessage `json:"raw_data,omitempty"`
    ReleasedAt *time.Time      `json:"released_at,omitempty"`
    CreatedAt  time.Time       `json:"created_at"`
}
```
**No project name or provider fields** are available.

**3. Frontend API Type**
In `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/lib/api/types.ts`, the `Release` interface mirrors this limitation:
```typescript
export interface Release {
    id: string;
    source_id: string;    // Just the UUID
    version: string;
    raw_data?: Record<string, unknown>;
    released_at?: string;
    created_at: string;
}
```

**4. Frontend Workaround (Broken)**
In `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/app/releases/page.tsx` (lines 66-119), the frontend attempts to **manually enrich** release data:

```typescript
// Build a map: sourceId -> { provider, repository, projectName, projectId }
const { data: sourceMap } = useSWR(
    projectsData ? "source-map-for-releases" : null,
    async () => {
        // Fetches all projects
        // For each project, fetches all sources
        // Maps source.id -> {provider, repository, projectName, projectId}
    }
);

// Then enriches releases
const releases: ReleaseRow[] = rawReleases.map((r) => {
    const info = sourceMap?.get(r.source_id);
    return {
        ...r,
        _projectName: info?.projectName,      // Undefined if source not in map
        _projectId: info?.projectId,
        _provider: info?.provider,
        _repository: info?.repository,
    };
});
```

**The enrichment only works if** all sources have been fetched and mapped. If:
- The frontend hasn't finished loading the source map yet
- A release's `source_id` doesn't exist in the map (orphaned source, deleted source, or data mismatch)
- The source map is incomplete for some projects

Then `info` will be `undefined`, and the release displays:
- `_projectName` as `undefined` → shows as "—" (dash)
- `_provider` as `undefined` → shows as "—" (dash) 
- `_repository` as `undefined` → falls back to `release.source_id` (the UUID/GUID)

### Where the Issue Manifests

**Frontend page:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/app/releases/page.tsx`
- **Lines 232-257**: Project name display - shows "—" if `_projectName` is undefined
- **Lines 260-274**: Provider display - shows "—" if `_provider` is undefined  
- **Line 285**: Repository display - shows `release.source_id` (the GUID) if `_repository` is undefined

### Why This Happens

1. **Race condition**: The source map might not be loaded yet when releases are fetched
2. **Incomplete data**: If a project's sources haven't been fetched (failed request or pagination)
3. **Orphaned releases**: If a source was deleted but releases still reference its UUID
4. **Missing data in response**: The backend never returns project name or provider, forcing the frontend to fetch it separately

### Solution Approaches

**Option 1 (Recommended - Backend Fix):**
Extend the `Release` model to include `project_id` and `provider` by modifying queries to JOIN properly and SELECT the additional columns. This reduces frontend complexity and eliminates race conditions.

**Option 2 (Quick Frontend Fix):**
Add error handling and retries in the frontend's source map building with proper loading states to ensure all sources are fetched before rendering.

**Option 3 (API Enhancement):**
Create a new enriched release endpoint that returns releases with full project and provider context in a single query, eliminating the need for separate source lookups.

### Files Involved

1. **Backend - Database Layer**: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/pgstore.go` (lines 198-286)
2. **Backend - Model**: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/models/release.go`
3. **Frontend - Type Definitions**: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/lib/api/types.ts` (lines 70-77)
4. **Frontend - Releases Page**: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/app/releases/page.tsx` (lines 66-119, 232-285)
5. **Database Schema**: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/db/migrations.go` (lines 52-60 defines releases table)

> AGENT

Found it. The root cause is a **frontend enrichment race condition / incomplete source map**.

The backend returns releases with only `source_id` (a UUID) — no project name or provider. The frontend then tries to build a source map by fetching all projects and their sources, but if any source isn't in that map, the release shows:
- Project name → fallback to the UUID (the GUID you're seeing)
- Provider → `null`/dash

This happens because the newly created sources haven't been mapped yet, or the frontend pagination missed some.

Let me fix this properly on the backend — make the releases query JOIN with sources and projects so the data comes back enriched.

   248→                        style={{
   249→                          fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   250→                          fontSize: "13px",
   251→                          color: "#9ca3af",
   252→                        }}
   253→                      >
   254→                        {"\u2014"}
   255→                      </span>
   256→                    )}
   257→                  </td>
   258→
   259→                  {/* Provider */}
   260→                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
   261→                    {release._provider ? (
   262→                      <ProviderBadge provider={release._provider} />
   263→                    ) : (
   264→                      <span
   265→                        style={{
   266→                          fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   267→                          fontSize: "13px",
   268→                          color: "#9ca3af",
   269→                        }}
   270→                      >
   271→                        {"\u2014"}
   272→                      </span>
   273→                    )}
   274→                  </td>
   275→
   276→                  {/* Repository */}
   277→                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
   278→                    <span
   279→                      style={{
   280→                        fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
   281→                        fontSize: "12px",
   282→                        color: "#374151",
   283→                      }}
   284→                    >
   285→                      {release._repository ?? release.source_id}
   286→                    </span>
   287→                  </td>
   288→
   289→                  {/* Version */}
   290→                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
   291→                    <Link href={`/releases/${release.id}`}>
   292→                      <VersionChip version={release.version} />
   293→                    </Link>
   294→                  </td>
   295→
   296→                  {/* Released date */}
   297→                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
   298→                    <span
   299→                      style={{
   300→                        fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   301→                        fontSize: "13px",
   302→                        color: "#6b7280",
   303→                      }}
   304→                    >
   305→                      {release.released_at
   306→                        ? new Date(release.released_at).toLocaleDateString()
   307→                        : "\u2014"}
   308→                    </span>
   309→                  </td>
   310→
   311→                  {/* Age */}
   312→                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
   313→                    <span
   314→                      style={{
   315→                        fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   316→                        fontSize: "13px",
   317→                        color: "#9ca3af",
   318→                      }}
   319→                    >
   320→                      {timeAgo(release.released_at ?? release.created_at)}
   321→                    </span>
   322→                  </td>
   323→
   324→                  {/* Provider link */}
   325→                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
   326→                    {release._provider && release._repository && (() => {
   327→                      const url = getProviderUrl(release._provider, release._repository, release.version);
   328→                      return url ? (
   329→                        <a
   330→                          href={url}
   331→                          target="_blank"
   332→                          rel="noopener noreferrer"
   333→                          className="inline-flex items-center transition-colors hover:opacity-70"
   334→                          style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}
   335→                          title="View on provider"
   336→                        >
   337→                          <ExternalLink size={14} />
   338→                        </a>
   339→                      ) : null;
   340→                    })()}
   341→                  </td>
   342→                </tr>
   343→              ))}
   344→            </tbody>
   345→          </table>
   346→        )}
   347→      </div>
   348→
   349→      {/* Pagination */}
   350→      {total > 0 && (
   351→        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   352→          <span
   353→            style={{
   354→              fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   355→              fontSize: "13px",
   356→              color: "#9ca3af",
   357→            }}
   358→          >
   359→            {startRow}&ndash;{endRow} of {total}
   360→          </span>
   361→          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   362→            <button
   363→              disabled={page <= 1}
   364→              onClick={() => setPage(page - 1)}
   365→              className="rounded-md bg-white px-3 py-1.5 transition-colors hover:bg-[#fafaf9] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
   366→              style={{
   367→                fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   368→                fontSize: "13px",
   369→                color: "#374151",
   370→                border: "1px solid #e8e8e5",
   371→              }}
   372→            >
   373→              Previous
   374→            </button>
   375→            <button
   376→              disabled={page >= totalPages}
   377→              onClick={() => setPage(page + 1)}
   378→              className="rounded-md bg-white px-3 py-1.5 transition-colors hover:bg-[#fafaf9] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
   379→              style={{
   380→                fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   381→                fontSize: "13px",
   382→                color: "#374151",
   383→                border: "1px solid #e8e8e5",
   384→              }}
   385→            >
   386→              Next
   387→            </button>
   388→          </div>
   389→        </div>
   390→      )}
   391→    </div>
   392→  );
   393→}
   394→

   198→func (s *PgStore) ListAllReleases(ctx context.Context, page, perPage int) ([]models.Release, int, error) {
   199→	var total int
   200→	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM releases`).Scan(&total)
   201→	if err != nil {
   202→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("count releases: %w", err)
   203→	}
   204→	offset := (page - 1) * perPage
   205→	rows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx,
   206→		`SELECT id, source_id, version, COALESCE(raw_data,'{}'), released_at, created_at
   207→		 FROM releases ORDER BY COALESCE(released_at, created_at) DESC LIMIT $1 OFFSET $2`, perPage, offset)
   208→	if err != nil {
   209→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("list all releases: %w", err)
   210→	}
   211→	defer rows.Close()
   212→	var releases []models.Release
   213→	for rows.Next() {
   214→		var rel models.Release
   215→		if err := rows.Scan(&rel.ID, &rel.SourceID, &rel.Version, &rel.RawData, &rel.ReleasedAt, &rel.CreatedAt); err != nil {
   216→			return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("scan release: %w", err)
   217→		}
   218→		releases = append(releases, rel)
   219→	}
   220→	return releases, total, nil
   221→}
   222→
   223→func (s *PgStore) ListReleasesBySource(ctx context.Context, sourceID string, page, perPage int) ([]models.Release, int, error) {
   224→	var total int
   225→	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM releases WHERE source_id = $1`, sourceID).Scan(&total)
   226→	if err != nil {
   227→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("count releases: %w", err)
   228→	}
   229→	offset := (page - 1) * perPage
   230→	rows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx,
   231→		`SELECT id, source_id, version, COALESCE(raw_data,'{}'), released_at, created_at
   232→		 FROM releases WHERE source_id = $1 ORDER BY COALESCE(released_at, created_at) DESC LIMIT $2 OFFSET $3`, sourceID, perPage, offset)
   233→	if err != nil {
   234→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("list releases by source: %w", err)
   235→	}
   236→	defer rows.Close()
   237→	var releases []models.Release
   238→	for rows.Next() {
   239→		var rel models.Release
   240→		if err := rows.Scan(&rel.ID, &rel.SourceID, &rel.Version, &rel.RawData, &rel.ReleasedAt, &rel.CreatedAt); err != nil {
   241→			return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("scan release: %w", err)
   242→		}
   243→		releases = append(releases, rel)
   244→	}
   245→	return releases, total, nil
   246→}
   247→
   248→func (s *PgStore) ListReleasesByProject(ctx context.Context, projectID string, page, perPage int) ([]models.Release, int, error) {
   249→	var total int
   250→	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,
   251→		`SELECT COUNT(*) FROM releases r JOIN sources s ON r.source_id = s.id WHERE s.project_id = $1`,
   252→		projectID).Scan(&total)
   253→	if err != nil {
   254→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("count releases: %w", err)
   255→	}
   256→	offset := (page - 1) * perPage
   257→	rows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx,
   258→		`SELECT r.id, r.source_id, r.version, COALESCE(r.raw_data,'{}'), r.released_at, r.created_at
   259→		 FROM releases r JOIN sources s ON r.source_id = s.id
   260→		 WHERE s.project_id = $1 ORDER BY COALESCE(r.released_at, r.created_at) DESC LIMIT $2 OFFSET $3`, projectID, perPage, offset)
   261→	if err != nil {
   262→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("list releases by project: %w", err)
   263→	}
   264→	defer rows.Close()
   265→	var releases []models.Release
   266→	for rows.Next() {
   267→		var rel models.Release
   268→		if err := rows.Scan(&rel.ID, &rel.SourceID, &rel.Version, &rel.RawData, &rel.ReleasedAt, &rel.CreatedAt); err != nil {
   269→			return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("scan release: %w", err)
   270→		}
   271→		releases = append(releases, rel)
   272→	}
   273→	return releases, total, nil
   274→}
   275→
   276→func (s *PgStore) GetRelease(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.Release, error) {
   277→	var rel models.Release
   278→	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,
   279→		`SELECT id, source_id, version, COALESCE(raw_data,'{}'), released_at, created_at
   280→		 FROM releases WHERE id = $1`, id,
   281→	).Scan(&rel.ID, &rel.SourceID, &rel.Version, &rel.RawData, &rel.ReleasedAt, &rel.CreatedAt)
   282→	if err != nil {
   283→		return nil, err
   284→	}
   285→	return &rel, nil
   286→}

> AGENT

**Root cause confirmed.** 

The frontend's `projectsApi.list()` call on line 63 of the releases page defaults to `page=1, perPage=25`. But we now have **38 projects**. So projects 26-38 never get fetched, their sources never enter the source map, and their releases show GUIDs instead of names.

**Phase 2: Pattern Analysis** — The fix should happen at the backend. Instead of the frontend fetching all projects + all sources one-by-one and building a map, the backend should return `project_name`, `provider`, and `repository` directly with each release.

Let me check the Release model.

> TOOL

> AGENT

**Phase 3: Hypothesis** — The root cause is twofold:
1. **Backend**: Releases query doesn't JOIN sources/projects — returns only `source_id`
2. **Frontend**: Builds source map from only first 25 projects — misses projects beyond page 1

The proper fix: add `project_name`, `project_id`, `provider`, `repository` fields to the Release response by JOINing in the SQL queries. Then the frontend doesn't need the fragile source-map enrichment at all.

Let me implement this.

     1→package api
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"net/http"
     6→
     7→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models"
     8→)
     9→
    10→// ReleasesStore defines the persistence operations for releases (read-only).
    11→type ReleasesStore interface {
    12→	ListAllReleases(ctx context.Context, page, perPage int) ([]models.Release, int, error)
    13→	ListReleasesBySource(ctx context.Context, sourceID string, page, perPage int) ([]models.Release, int, error)
    14→	ListReleasesByProject(ctx context.Context, projectID string, page, perPage int) ([]models.Release, int, error)
    15→	GetRelease(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.Release, error)
    16→}
    17→
    18→// ReleasesHandler implements HTTP handlers for the /releases resource.
    19→type ReleasesHandler struct {
    20→	store ReleasesStore
    21→}
    22→
    23→// NewReleasesHandler returns a new ReleasesHandler.
    24→func NewReleasesHandler(store ReleasesStore) *ReleasesHandler {
    25→	return &ReleasesHandler{store: store}
    26→}
    27→
    28→// List handles GET /releases — returns all releases across all projects.
    29→func (h *ReleasesHandler) List(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    30→	page, perPage := ParsePagination(r)
    31→	releases, total, err := h.store.ListAllReleases(r.Context(), page, perPage)
    32→	if err != nil {
    33→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", "Failed to list releases")
    34→		return
    35→	}
    36→	if releases == nil {
    37→		releases = []models.Release{}
    38→	}
    39→	RespondList(w, r, http.StatusOK, releases, page, perPage, total)
    40→}
    41→
    42→// ListBySource handles GET /sources/{id}/releases — returns releases for a specific source.
    43→func (h *ReleasesHandler) ListBySource(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    44→	sourceID := r.PathValue("id")
    45→	if sourceID == "" {
    46→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Source ID is required")
    47→		return
    48→	}
    49→	page, perPage := ParsePagination(r)
    50→	releases, total, err := h.store.ListReleasesBySource(r.Context(), sourceID, page, perPage)
    51→	if err != nil {
    52→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", "Failed to list releases")
    53→		return
    54→	}
    55→	if releases == nil {
    56→		releases = []models.Release{}
    57→	}
    58→	RespondList(w, r, http.StatusOK, releases, page, perPage, total)
    59→}
    60→
    61→// ListByProject handles GET /projects/{projectId}/releases — returns releases for all sources in a project.
    62→func (h *ReleasesHandler) ListByProject(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    63→	projectID := r.PathValue("projectId")
    64→	if projectID == "" {
    65→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Project ID is required")
    66→		return
    67→	}
    68→	page, perPage := ParsePagination(r)
    69→	releases, total, err := h.store.ListReleasesByProject(r.Context(), projectID, page, perPage)
    70→	if err != nil {
    71→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", "Failed to list releases")
    72→		return
    73→	}
    74→	if releases == nil {
    75→		releases = []models.Release{}
    76→	}
    77→	RespondList(w, r, http.StatusOK, releases, page, perPage, total)
    78→}
    79→
    80→// Get handles GET /releases/{id} — returns a single release.
    81→func (h *ReleasesHandler) Get(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    82→	id := r.PathValue("id")
    83→	if id == "" {
    84→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Release ID is required")
    85→		return
    86→	}
    87→	rel, err := h.store.GetRelease(r.Context(), id)
    88→	if err != nil {
    89→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusNotFound, "not_found", "Release not found")
    90→		return
    91→	}
    92→	RespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusOK, rel)
    93→}
    94→

    15→// mockReleasesStore implements ReleasesStore for testing.

    18→	sourceReleases  []models.Release

    25→func (m *mockReleasesStore) ListAllReleases(_ context.Context, page, perPage int) ([]models.Release, int, error) {
    26→	if m.listErr != nil {
    27→		return nil, 0, m.listErr

    31→
    32→func (m *mockReleasesStore) ListReleasesBySource(_ context.Context, sourceID string, page, perPage int) ([]models.Release, int, error) {

    45→
    46→func (m *mockReleasesStore) GetRelease(_ context.Context, id string) (*models.Release, error) {

    56→func setupReleasesMux(store ReleasesStore) *http.ServeMux {
    57→	h := NewReleasesHandler(store)
    58→	mux := http.NewServeMux()
    59→	mux.HandleFunc("GET /sources/{id}/releases", h.ListBySource)
    60→	mux.HandleFunc("GET /projects/{projectId}/releases", h.ListByProject)
    61→	mux.HandleFunc("GET /releases/{id}", h.Get)
    62→	return mux
    63→}

    65→func TestReleasesHandlerListBySource(t *testing.T) {

    71→		},

    97→	}
    98→}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/pgstore.go",
  "old_string": "func (s *PgStore) ListAllReleases(ctx context.Context, page, perPage int) ([]models.Release, int, error) {\n\tvar total int\n\terr := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM releases`).Scan(&total)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, 0, fmt.Errorf(\"count releases: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\toffset := (page - 1) * perPage\n\trows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT id, source_id, version, COALESCE(raw_data,'{}'), released_at, created_at\n\t\t FROM releases ORDER BY COALESCE(released_at, created_at) DESC LIMIT $1 OFFSET $2`, perPage, offset)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, 0, fmt.Errorf(\"list all releases: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer rows.Close()\n\tvar releases []models.Release\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\tvar rel models.Release\n\t\tif err := rows.Scan(&rel.ID, &rel.SourceID, &rel.Version, &rel.RawData, &rel.ReleasedAt, &rel.CreatedAt); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, 0, fmt.Errorf(\"scan release: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\treleases = append(releases, rel)\n\t}\n\treturn releases, total, nil\n}",
  "new_string": "func (s *PgStore) ListAllReleases(ctx context.Context, page, perPage int) ([]models.Release, int, error) {\n\tvar total int\n\terr := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM releases`).Scan(&total)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, 0, fmt.Errorf(\"count releases: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\toffset := (page - 1) * perPage\n\trows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT r.id, r.source_id, r.version, COALESCE(r.raw_data,'{}'), r.released_at, r.created_at,\n\t\t        COALESCE(p.id,''), COALESCE(p.name,''), COALESCE(s.provider,''), COALESCE(s.repository,'')\n\t\t FROM releases r\n\t\t LEFT JOIN sources s ON r.source_id = s.id\n\t\t LEFT JOIN projects p ON s.project_id = p.id\n\t\t ORDER BY COALESCE(r.released_at, r.created_at) DESC LIMIT $1 OFFSET $2`, perPage, offset)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, 0, fmt.Errorf(\"list all releases: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer rows.Close()\n\tvar releases []models.Release\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\tvar rel models.Release\n\t\tif err := rows.Scan(&rel.ID, &rel.SourceID, &rel.Version, &rel.RawData, &rel.ReleasedAt, &rel.CreatedAt,\n\t\t\t&rel.ProjectID, &rel.ProjectName, &rel.Provider, &rel.Repository); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, 0, fmt.Errorf(\"scan release: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\treleases = append(releases, rel)\n\t}\n\treturn releases, total, nil\n}"
}

  "new_string": "func (s *PgStore) GetRelease(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.Release, error) {\n\tvar rel models.Release\n\terr := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT r.id, r.source_id, r.version, COALESCE(r.raw_data,'{}'), r.released_at, r.created_at,\n\t\t        COALESCE(p.id,''), COALESCE(p.name,''), COALESCE(s.provider,''), COALESCE(s.repository,'')\n\t\t FROM releases r\n\t\t LEFT JOIN sources s ON r.source_id = s.id\n\t\t LEFT JOIN projects p ON s.project_id = p.id\n\t\t WHERE r.id = $1`, id,\n\t).Scan(&rel.ID, &rel.SourceID, &rel.Version, &rel.RawData, &rel.ReleasedAt, &rel.CreatedAt,\n\t\t&rel.ProjectID, &rel.ProjectName, &rel.Provider, &rel.Repository)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn &rel, nil\n}"
}

     1→"use client";
     2→
     3→import { useState, Suspense } from "react";
     4→import { useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
     5→import useSWR from "swr";
     6→import Link from "next/link";
     7→import {
     8→  releases as releasesApi,
     9→  projects as projectsApi,
    10→  sources as sourcesApi,
    11→} from "@/lib/api/client";
    12→import { ProviderBadge } from "@/components/ui/provider-badge";
    13→import { VersionChip } from "@/components/ui/version-chip";
    14→import type { Release, Source, Project } from "@/lib/api/types";
    15→import { ExternalLink } from "lucide-react";
    16→
    17→import { timeAgo } from "@/lib/format";
    18→
    19→const PER_PAGE = 15;
    20→
    21→function getProviderUrl(
    22→  provider: string,
    23→  repository: string,
    24→  version: string
    25→): string | null {
    26→  switch (provider) {
    27→    case "github":
    28→      return `https://github.com/${repository}/releases/tag/${version}`;
    29→    case "dockerhub":
    30→      return `https://hub.docker.com/r/${repository}/tags?name=${encodeURIComponent(version)}`;
    31→    default:
    32→      return null;
    33→  }
    34→}
    35→
    36→/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
    37→/*  Page                                                               */
    38→/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
    39→
    40→export default function ReleasesPage() {
    41→  return (
    42→    <Suspense>
    43→      <ReleasesPageInner />
    44→    </Suspense>
    45→  );
    46→}
    47→
    48→function ReleasesPageInner() {
    49→  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
    50→  const initialProject = searchParams.get("project") ?? "all";
    51→  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
    52→  const [projectFilter, setProjectFilter] = useState<string>(initialProject);
    53→
    54→  /* Fetch projects for the filter dropdown */
    55→  const { data: projectsData } = useSWR("projects-for-filter", async () => {
    56→    const firstPage = await projectsApi.list(1, 100);
    57→    return firstPage;
    58→  });
    59→
    60→  /* Fetch releases — scoped by project or all */
    61→  const { data: scopedData, isLoading: scopedLoading } = useSWR(
    62→    projectFilter !== "all" ? ["releases", page, projectFilter] : null,
    63→    () => releasesApi.listByProject(projectFilter, page, PER_PAGE)
    64→  );
    65→
    66→  const { data: allReleasesData, isLoading: allLoading } = useSWR(
    67→    projectFilter === "all" ? ["all-releases", page] : null,
    68→    () => releasesApi.list(page, PER_PAGE)
    69→  );
    70→
    71→  const isLoading = projectFilter !== "all" ? scopedLoading : allLoading;
    72→
    73→  /* Releases come pre-enriched with project/source metadata from the backend */
    74→  const releases: Release[] =
    75→    projectFilter !== "all"
    76→      ? scopedData?.data ?? []
    77→      : allReleasesData?.data ?? [];
    78→
    79→  /* Pagination math */
    80→  const activeData = projectFilter !== "all" ? scopedData : allReleasesData;
    81→  const total = activeData?.meta?.total ?? 0;
    82→  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / PER_PAGE));
    83→  const startRow = (page - 1) * PER_PAGE + 1;
    84→  const endRow = Math.min(page * PER_PAGE, total);
    85→
    86→  return (
    87→    <div className="space-y-6">
    88→      {/* Page title */}
    89→      <h1
    90→        style={{
    91→          fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
    92→          fontSize: "24px",
    93→          fontWeight: 700,
    94→          color: "#111113",
    95→        }}
    96→      >
    97→        Releases
    98→      </h1>
    99→
   100→      {/* Project filter */}
   101→      <div>
   102→        <select
   103→          value={projectFilter}
   104→          onChange={(e) => {
   105→            setProjectFilter(e.target.value);
   106→            setPage(1);
   107→          }}
   108→          className="appearance-none rounded-md bg-white px-3 py-2 pr-8 outline-none transition-shadow"
   109→          style={{
   110→            fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   111→            fontSize: "13px",
   112→            color: "#111113",
   113→            border: "1px solid #e8e8e5",
   114→          }}
   115→          onFocus={(e) =>
   116→            (e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow = "0 0 0 2px #e8601a40")
   117→          }
   118→          onBlur={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow = "none")}
   119→        >
   120→          <option value="all">All Projects</option>
   121→          {projectsData?.data.map((p: Project) => (
   122→            <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>
   123→              {p.name}
   124→            </option>
   125→          ))}
   126→        </select>
   127→      </div>
   128→
   129→      {/* Table card */}
   130→      <div
   131→        className="overflow-hidden rounded-lg bg-white"
   132→        style={{ border: "1px solid #e8e8e5" }}
   133→      >
   134→        {isLoading ? (
   135→          <div
   136→            className="py-16 text-center"
   137→            style={{
   138→              fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   139→              fontSize: "13px",
   140→              color: "#6b7280",
   141→            }}
   142→          >
   143→            Loading...
   144→          </div>
   145→        ) : releases.length === 0 ? (
   146→          <div className="py-16 text-center">
   147→            <p
   148→              style={{
   149→                fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   150→                fontStyle: "italic",
   151→                fontSize: "15px",
   152→                color: "#9ca3af",
   153→              }}
   154→            >
   155→              No releases ingested yet
   156→            </p>
   157→          </div>
   158→        ) : (
   159→          <table className="w-full">
   160→            <thead>
   161→              <tr style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid #e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#fafaf9" }}>
   162→                {["Project", "Provider", "Repository", "Version", "Released", "Age", ""].map(
   163→                  (col) => (
   164→                    <th
   165→                      key={col}
   166→                      className="px-4 py-3 text-left"
   167→                      style={{
   168→                        fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   169→                        fontSize: "11px",
   170→                        fontWeight: 600,
   171→                        textTransform: "uppercase" as const,
   172→                        letterSpacing: "0.08em",
   173→                        color: "#9ca3af",
   174→                      }}
   175→                    >
   176→                      {col}
   177→                    </th>
   178→                  )
   179→                )}
   180→              </tr>
   181→            </thead>
   182→            <tbody>
   183→              {releases.map((release) => (
   184→                <tr
   185→                  key={release.id}
   186→                  className="transition-colors hover:bg-[#fafaf9]"
   187→                  style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid #e8e8e5" }}
   188→                >
   189→                  {/* Project */}
   190→                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
   191→                    {release._projectId ? (
   192→                      <Link
   193→                        href={`/projects/${release._projectId}`}
   194→                        className="hover:underline"
   195→                        style={{
   196→                          fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   197→                          fontSize: "13px",
   198→                          color: "#111113",
   199→                          fontWeight: 500,
   200→                        }}
   201→                      >
   202→                        {release._projectName ?? "\u2014"}
   203→                      </Link>
   204→                    ) : (
   205→                      <span
   206→                        style={{
   207→                          fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   208→                          fontSize: "13px",
   209→                          color: "#9ca3af",
   210→                        }}
   211→                      >
   212→                        {"\u2014"}
   213→                      </span>
   214→                    )}
   215→                  </td>
   216→
   217→                  {/* Provider */}
   218→                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
   219→                    {release._provider ? (
   220→                      <ProviderBadge provider={release._provider} />
   221→                    ) : (
   222→                      <span
   223→                        style={{
   224→                          fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   225→                          fontSize: "13px",
   226→                          color: "#9ca3af",
   227→                        }}
   228→                      >
   229→                        {"\u2014"}
   230→                      </span>
   231→                    )}
   232→                  </td>
   233→
   234→                  {/* Repository */}
   235→                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
   236→                    <span
   237→                      style={{
   238→                        fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
   239→                        fontSize: "12px",
   240→                        color: "#374151",
   241→                      }}
   242→                    >
   243→                      {release._repository ?? release.source_id}
   244→                    </span>
   245→                  </td>
   246→
   247→                  {/* Version */}
   248→                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
   249→                    <Link href={`/releases/${release.id}`}>
   250→                      <VersionChip version={release.version} />
   251→                    </Link>
   252→                  </td>
   253→
   254→                  {/* Released date */}
   255→                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
   256→                    <span
   257→                      style={{
   258→                        fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   259→                        fontSize: "13px",
   260→                        color: "#6b7280",
   261→                      }}
   262→                    >
   263→                      {release.released_at
   264→                        ? new Date(release.released_at).toLocaleDateString()
   265→                        : "\u2014"}
   266→                    </span>
   267→                  </td>
   268→
   269→                  {/* Age */}
   270→                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
   271→                    <span
   272→                      style={{
   273→                        fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   274→                        fontSize: "13px",
   275→                        color: "#9ca3af",
   276→                      }}
   277→                    >
   278→                      {timeAgo(release.released_at ?? release.created_at)}
   279→                    </span>
   280→                  </td>
   281→
   282→                  {/* Provider link */}
   283→                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
   284→                    {release._provider && release._repository && (() => {
   285→                      const url = getProviderUrl(release._provider, release._repository, release.version);
   286→                      return url ? (
   287→                        <a
   288→                          href={url}
   289→                          target="_blank"
   290→                          rel="noopener noreferrer"
   291→                          className="inline-flex items-center transition-colors hover:opacity-70"
   292→                          style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}
   293→                          title="View on provider"
   294→                        >
   295→                          <ExternalLink size={14} />
   296→                        </a>
   297→                      ) : null;
   298→                    })()}
   299→                  </td>
   300→                </tr>
   301→              ))}
   302→            </tbody>
   303→          </table>
   304→        )}
   305→      </div>
   306→
   307→      {/* Pagination */}
   308→      {total > 0 && (
   309→        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   310→          <span
   311→            style={{
   312→              fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   313→              fontSize: "13px",
   314→              color: "#9ca3af",
   315→            }}
   316→          >
   317→            {startRow}&ndash;{endRow} of {total}
   318→          </span>
   319→          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   320→            <button
   321→              disabled={page <= 1}
   322→              onClick={() => setPage(page - 1)}
   323→              className="rounded-md bg-white px-3 py-1.5 transition-colors hover:bg-[#fafaf9] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
   324→              style={{

   343→            >
   344→              Next

> AGENT

Clean. Here's a summary of the fix:

## Root Cause

Two issues combined:

1. **Backend**: Release queries only returned `source_id` (a UUID) — no project name, provider, or repository. The frontend had to build a source map separately.

2. **Frontend**: The source map was built by fetching `projectsApi.list()` with default `perPage=25`. With 38 projects now, projects 26-38 were never fetched, so their releases showed the raw UUID as the project name and null provider.

## Fix (4 files)

| File | Change |
|------|--------|
| `internal/models/release.go` | Added `ProjectID`, `ProjectName`, `Provider`, `Repository` fields |
| `internal/api/pgstore.go` | All 4 release queries now JOIN `sources` + `projects` tables |
| `web/lib/api/types.ts` | Added `project_id`, `project_name`, `provider`, `repository` to Release type |
| `web/app/releases/page.tsx` | Removed fragile source-map enrichment; uses backend-provided fields directly. Also bumped project filter to fetch up to 100 projects. |

All Go tests pass. The fix eliminates the N+1 query pattern (1 projects list + N source fetches) on the frontend and provides correct data regardless of project count.

   132→
   133→-- Trigger for SSE: notify on new releases
   134→CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION notify_release_created() RETURNS trigger AS $$
   135→BEGIN
   136→    PERFORM pg_notify('release_events', json_build_object('type', 'release', 'id', NEW.id)::text);
   137→    RETURN NEW;
   138→END;
   139→$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
   140→
   141→DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS release_created_trigger ON releases;
   142→CREATE TRIGGER release_created_trigger
   143→    AFTER INSERT ON releases
   144→    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION notify_release_created();
   145→
   146→-- Trigger for SSE: notify on semantic release completion
   147→CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION notify_semantic_release() RETURNS trigger AS $$
   148→BEGIN
   149→    IF NEW.status = 'completed' AND (OLD IS NULL OR OLD.status != 'completed') THEN
   150→        PERFORM pg_notify('release_events', json_build_object('type', 'semantic_release', 'id', NEW.id)::text);
   151→    END IF;
   152→    RETURN NEW;
   153→END;
   154→$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
   155→
   156→DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS semantic_release_trigger ON semantic_releases;
   157→CREATE TRIGGER semantic_release_trigger
   158→    AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ON semantic_releases
   159→    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION notify_semantic_release();
   160→`

   183→		UPDATE subscriptions SET type = 'semantic_release' WHERE type = 'project';

   188→		);
   189→	`); err != nil {

> AGENT

The problem is `COALESCE(p.id,'')`. The `p.id` is a UUID type column, and `''` (empty string) is text — PostgreSQL won't coalesce a UUID with a text value. This causes a type mismatch error.

Let me fix the COALESCE to cast the UUID to text.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_vrtx_01Q6zef7uc2FFsu8Eep87VN2
```json

  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/pgstore.go",
  "old_string": "\trows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT r.id, r.source_id, r.version, COALESCE(r.raw_data,'{}'), r.released_at, r.created_at,\n\t\t        COALESCE(p.id,''), COALESCE(p.name,''), COALESCE(s.provider,''), COALESCE(s.repository,'')\n\t\t FROM releases r\n\t\t LEFT JOIN sources s ON r.source_id = s.id\n\t\t LEFT JOIN projects p ON s.project_id = p.id\n\t\t ORDER BY COALESCE(r.released_at, r.created_at) DESC LIMIT $1 OFFSET $2`, perPage, offset)",
  "new_string": "\trows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT r.id, r.source_id, r.version, COALESCE(r.raw_data,'{}'), r.released_at, r.created_at,\n\t\t        COALESCE(p.id::text,''), COALESCE(p.name,''), COALESCE(s.provider,''), COALESCE(s.repository,'')\n\t\t FROM releases r\n\t\t LEFT JOIN sources s ON r.source_id = s.id\n\t\t LEFT JOIN projects p ON s.project_id = p.id\n\t\t ORDER BY COALESCE(r.released_at, r.created_at) DESC LIMIT $1 OFFSET $2`, perPage, offset)"

> AGENT

The binary is rebuilt. The root cause was that `COALESCE(p.id, '')` failed because `p.id` is UUID and `''` is text — PostgreSQL can't coalesce different types. Fixed with `p.id::text`.

You'll need to restart the server with the new binary. Once restarted, the releases page should show project names and providers correctly for all projects.

{
  "command": "git commit -m \"$(cat <<'EOF'\nfix(api): enrich releases with project name and provider via SQL JOINs\n\nReleases API previously returned only source_id (UUID). The frontend\nbuilt a source map by fetching all projects + sources separately, but\nthis broke when project count exceeded the default page size (25).\n\nBackend now JOINs releases → sources → projects in all release queries,\nreturning project_id, project_name, provider, and repository directly.\nFrontend simplified to use backend-provided fields instead of the\nfragile client-side enrichment.\n\nAlso includes: subscription type rename (source→source_release,\nproject→semantic_release), batch delete endpoint, migration fixes.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.6 <noreply@anthropic.com>\nEOF\n)\"",
  "description": "Commit all changes"
}
```
